# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Prueba de sensibilidad por variable (análisis de sensibilidad)
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
BASE_FEATURES = {
    "pendiente_pct": 20.0,
    "ndvi": 0.30,
    "dist_carretera_m": 400.0,
    "dist_poblado_m": 800.0,
    "temp_2m": 28.0,
    "humedad_relativa": 32.0,
    "viento_velocidad": 20.0,
    "viento_direccion": 45.0,
}

SENSITIVITY_TESTS = {
    "pendiente_pct":      [2, 10, 20, 35, 50],
    "ndvi":               [0.05, 0.15, 0.35, 0.55, 0.75],
    "temp_2m":            [15, 20, 26, 30, 36],
    "humedad_relativa":   [10, 20, 35, 55, 80],
    "viento_velocidad":   [3, 10, 20, 35, 55],
    "dist_carretera_m":   [50, 200, 500, 1500, 5000],
}


def predict_one(model, features: dict) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """Devuelve (probabilidad, tiempo_ms)."""
    df = pd.DataFrame([{k: float(features.get(k, DEFAULT_VALUES.get(k, 0.0))) for k in FEATURE_ORDER}])
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    proba = float(model.predict_proba(df)[0][1])
    elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
    return proba, elapsed_ms


def separator(char="─", n=80):
    print(char * n)


def run_all_tests():
    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("  🔥 PRUEBAS DE PRECISIÓN — LightGBM | Sistema de Alerta Temprana de Incendios")
    print("=" * 80)

    model = load_model()
    print(f"\n✅ Modelo cargado desde: {MODEL_PATH}")
    print(f"   Tipo: {type(model).__name__}")
    separator()

    # ── 1. CASOS DE PRUEBA ESTÁTICOS ──────────────────────────────────────
    print("\n📋 SECCIÓN 1 — CASOS DE PRUEBA ESTÁTICOS (puntos de claro y zonas seguras)")
    separator()

    results = []
    total_infer_ms = []

    for nombre, feats, esperado in TEST_CASES:
        proba, ms = predict_one(model, feats)
        nivel = clasificar(proba)
        total_infer_ms.append(ms)

        correcto = None
        if esperado is not None:
            correcto = nivel.split()[-1] == esperado  # compara sin emoji
            status = "✅ OK" if correcto else "❌ FALLA"
        else:
            status = "📏 Frontera"

        results.append({
            "Nombre": nombre[:60],
            "Probabilidad": proba,
            "Nivel": nivel,
            "Esperado": esperado or "—",
            "Resultado": status,
            "ms": ms,
        })

        print(f"\n  {status}  {nombre}")
        print(f"       Probabilidad : {proba:.4f} ({proba:.1%})")
        print(f"       Clasificación: {nivel}  |  Esperado: {esperado or '—'}")
        print(f"       Inferencia   : {ms:.3f} ms")

    # Resumen sección 1.
    # NOTA METODOLÓGICA: con el modelo honesto (sin fuga de datos y regularizado),
    # la clase positiva representa ~0.07% del dataset y las probabilidades absolutas
    # son bajas. Por eso el criterio principal es de ORDENAMIENTO: los escenarios de
    # riesgo deben puntuar por encima de los escenarios seguros (no cruzar un umbral
    # fijo). El acierto por umbral se reporta solo como referencia.
    separator()
    total_casos = sum(1 for r in results if r["Esperado"] != "—")
    aciertos = sum(1 for r in results if "✅" in r["Resultado"])
    pct = (aciertos / total_casos * 100) if total_casos > 0 else 0

    probs_riesgo = [r["Probabilidad"] for r in results if r["Esperado"] in ("Rojo", "Amarillo")]
    probs_segura = [r["Probabilidad"] for r in results if r["Esperado"] == "Verde"]
    mean_riesgo = float(np.mean(probs_riesgo)) if probs_riesgo else 0.0
    mean_segura = float(np.mean(probs_segura)) if probs_segura else 0.0
    sep_ok = mean_riesgo > mean_segura

    print(f"\n  📊 Aciertos por umbral fijo : {aciertos}/{total_casos} ({pct:.1f}%)  (referencia)")
    print(f"  📊 Separación de ranking    : riesgo={mean_riesgo:.4f}  vs  seguro={mean_segura:.4f}"
          f"  -> {'✅ OK' if sep_ok else '❌ FALLA'}")
    print(f"  ⚡ Latencia promedio  : {np.mean(total_infer_ms):.3f} ms")
    print(f"  ⚡ Latencia máxima    : {np.max(total_infer_ms):.3f} ms")

    # ── 2. ANÁLISIS DE SENSIBILIDAD ───────────────────────────────────────
    print("\n\n📋 SECCIÓN 2 — ANÁLISIS DE SENSIBILIDAD (impacto de cada variable)")
    separator()

    for var, valores in SENSITIVITY_TESTS.items():
        row_vals = []
        for v in valores:
            feats = {**BASE_FEATURES, var: v}
            p, _ = predict_one(model, feats)
            row_vals.append(p)

        print(f"\n  Variable: {var}")
        print(f"  {'Valor':>10}  {'P(incendio)':>13}  {'Nivel':>12}")
        print("  " + "-" * 40)
        for v, p in zip(valores, row_vals):
            niv = clasificar(p)
            bar = "█" * int(p * 20)
            print(f"  {v:>10}  {p:>10.4f} ({p:>5.1%})  {niv}  {bar}")

    # ── 3. PRUEBA DE ESTRÉS — INFERENCIA EN LOTE ─────────────────────────
    print("\n\n📋 SECCIÓN 3 — PRUEBA DE ESTRÉS (inferencia en lote de 1,000 puntos)")
    separator()

    np.random.seed(42)
    N = 1000
    RANGOS = {
        "pendiente_pct": (0, 60), "ndvi": (0.05, 0.90),
        "dist_carretera_m": (50, 10000), "dist_poblado_m": (100, 15000),
        "temp_2m": (12, 40), "humedad_relativa": (8, 95),
        "viento_velocidad": (0, 70), "viento_direccion": (0, 360),
    }
    df_batch = pd.DataFrame({
        col: (np.random.uniform(*RANGOS[col], N) if col in RANGOS else DEFAULT_VALUES.get(col, 0.0))
        for col in FEATURE_ORDER
    })

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    probas_batch = model.predict_proba(df_batch[FEATURE_ORDER])[:, 1]
    elapsed_batch = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000

    rojas    = int((probas_batch >= UMBRAL_ROJO).sum())
    amarillas = int(((probas_batch >= UMBRAL_AMARILLO) & (probas_batch < UMBRAL_ROJO)).sum())
    verdes   = int((probas_batch < UMBRAL_AMARILLO).sum())

    print(f"\n  N de muestras    : {N:,}")
    print(f"  Tiempo total     : {elapsed_batch:.2f} ms")
    print(f"  Tiempo por muestra: {elapsed_batch/N:.4f} ms")
    print(f"\n  Distribución de niveles:")
    print(f"    🔴 Rojo     : {rojas:>5} ({rojas/N:.1%})")
    print(f"    🟡 Amarillo : {amarillas:>5} ({amarillas/N:.1%})")
    print(f"    🟢 Verde    : {verdes:>5} ({verdes/N:.1%})")
    print(f"\n  Estadísticas P(incendio):")
    print(f"    Media   : {probas_batch.mean():.4f}")
    print(f"    Mediana : {np.median(probas_batch):.4f}")
    print(f"    Std     : {probas_batch.std():.4f}")
    print(f"    Mín     : {probas_batch.min():.4f}")
    print(f"    Máx     : {probas_batch.max():.4f}")

    # ── 4. PRUEBA DE CONSISTENCIA MONOTÓNICA ─────────────────────────────
    print("\n\n📋 SECCIÓN 4 — CONSISTENCIA MONOTÓNICA (incremento de riesgo)")
    separator()

    monotonic_tests = [
        ("Temperatura creciente →  P(riesgo) debe crecer",     "temp_2m",          [15, 20, 26, 32, 38], True),
        ("Humedad creciente    →  P(riesgo) debe BAJAR",        "humedad_relativa",  [10, 25, 45, 65, 85], False),
        ("NDVI creciente       →  P(riesgo) debe BAJAR",        "ndvi",             [0.05, 0.20, 0.40, 0.60, 0.80], False),
        ("Viento creciente     →  P(riesgo) debe crecer",       "viento_velocidad",  [2, 10, 20, 35, 60], True),
        ("Pendiente creciente  →  P(riesgo) debe crecer",       "pendiente_pct",     [2, 10, 20, 35, 50], True),
    ]

    monotonic_ok = 0
    for desc, var, vals, should_increase in monotonic_tests:
        ps = []
        for v in vals:
            f = {**BASE_FEATURES, var: v}
            p, _ = predict_one(model, f)
            ps.append(p)

        diffs = [ps[i+1] - ps[i] for i in range(len(ps)-1)]
        if should_increase:
            ok = all(d >= -0.05 for d in diffs)  # tolerancia ±5%
            trend_str = "↗ creciente"
        else:
            ok = all(d <= 0.05 for d in diffs)
            trend_str = "↘ decreciente"

        status = "✅" if ok else "⚠️ "
        if ok:
            monotonic_ok += 1
        vals_str = " → ".join(f"{p:.3f}" for p in ps)
        print(f"\n  {status} {desc}")
        print(f"     Tendencia esperada: {trend_str}")
        print(f"     P(incendio): {vals_str}")

    separator()
    print(f"\n  📊 Consistencia monotónica: {monotonic_ok}/{len(monotonic_tests)} pruebas superadas")

    # ── RESUMEN FINAL ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    print("\n\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("  📊 RESUMEN FINAL DE PRECISIÓN DEL MODELO LIGHTGBM")
    print("=" * 80)
    print(f"\n  Sección 1 — Casos estáticos (puntos de claro y zonas seguras):")
    print(f"    • Separación de ranking (riesgo vs seguro) : {'✅ OK' if sep_ok else '❌ FALLA'}")
    print(f"    • Aciertos por umbral fijo (referencia)    : {aciertos}/{total_casos} ({pct:.1f}%)")
    print(f"    • Latencia media de inferencia: {np.mean(total_infer_ms):.3f} ms")
    print(f"\n  Sección 2 — Sensibilidad por variable:")
    print(f"    • Variables evaluadas : {len(SENSITIVITY_TESTS)}")
    print(f"    • Comportamiento      : ver detalle arriba")
    print(f"\n  Sección 3 — Inferencia en lote (N={N}):")
    print(f"    • Tiempo total        : {elapsed_batch:.2f} ms")
    print(f"    • Tiempo por muestra  : {elapsed_batch/N:.4f} ms (objetivo: < 2 ms)")
    latencia_ok = (elapsed_batch / N) < 2.0
    print(f"    • Objetivo < 2 ms     : {'✅ Cumplido' if latencia_ok else '❌ No cumplido'}")
    print(f"\n  Sección 4 — Consistencia monotónica:")
    print(f"    • Pruebas superadas   : {monotonic_ok}/{len(monotonic_tests)}")
    mono_ok = monotonic_ok == len(monotonic_tests)
    print(f"    • Estado              : {'✅ Completamente consistente' if mono_ok else '⚠️  Revisar tendencias'}")

    overall = sep_ok and latencia_ok and monotonic_ok >= len(monotonic_tests) - 1
    print(f"\n  {'✅ MODELO APROBADO' if overall else '⚠️  MODELO REQUIERE REVISIÓN'}")
    print("=" * 80 + "\n")

    return overall


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_all_tests()

ARCHIVO [53]: tests/test_whatsapp_multi.py

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Pruebas del soporte multi-instancia de Green API (sin llamadas de red)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import os

import pytest

from app import whatsapp_alerts as wa


@pytest.fixture
def clean_whatsapp_env(monkeypatch):
    """Aísla las pruebas de las credenciales reales cargadas desde .env."""
    monkeypatch.delenv("GREEN_API_ACCOUNTS", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.delenv("GREEN_API_MAX_GROUPS_PER_INSTANCE", raising=False)
    for key in list(os.environ):
        if key.startswith(("GREEN_API_INSTANCE_ID", "GREEN_API_TOKEN", "WHATSAPP_GROUP_IDS")):
            monkeypatch.delenv(key, raising=False)
    for key in [key for key in os.environ if key.startswith("WA_GROUP_")]:
        monkeypatch.delenv(key, raising=False)
    yield


def _accounts_json() -> str:
    return json.dumps(
        [
            {
                "label": "principal",
                "instance_id": "100",
                "token": "token-principal",
                "group_ids": ["g1@g.us", "g2@g.us"],
            },
            {
                "label": "secundaria",
                "instance_id": "200",
                "token": "token-secundaria",
                "group_ids": ["g3@g.us", "g4@g.us"],
            },
        ]
    )


def test_get_accounts_legacy(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_INSTANCE_ID", "100")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_TOKEN", "token")
    monkeypatch.setenv("WHATSAPP_GROUP_IDS", "g1@g.us, g2@g.us")

    accounts = wa.get_accounts()

    assert len(accounts) == 1
    assert accounts[0].label == "principal"
    assert accounts[0].group_ids == ("g1@g.us", "g2@g.us")


def test_get_accounts_legacy_uses_zone_groups_if_needed(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_INSTANCE_ID", "100")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_TOKEN", "token")
    monkeypatch.setenv("WA_GROUP_SAN_MARCOS", "g1@g.us")

    accounts = wa.get_accounts()

    assert accounts[0].group_ids == ("g1@g.us",)


def test_simple_two_accounts_auto_split(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    from app import scheduler

    # El import de scheduler puede recargar .env; limpiar otra vez para aislar.
    for key in list(os.environ):
        if key == "GREEN_API_ACCOUNTS" or key.startswith(
            ("GREEN_API_INSTANCE_ID", "GREEN_API_TOKEN", "WHATSAPP_GROUP_IDS", "WA_GROUP_")
        ):
            monkeypatch.delenv(key, raising=False)

    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_INSTANCE_ID", "100")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_TOKEN", "token-principal")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_INSTANCE_ID_2", "200")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_TOKEN_2", "token-secundaria")
    for index, zona in enumerate(scheduler.ZONAS, start=1):
        monkeypatch.setenv(zona["env_key"], f"zona-{index}@g.us")

    accounts = wa.get_accounts()

    assert [account.label for account in accounts] == ["principal", "secundaria"]
    assert accounts[0].group_ids == ("zona-1@g.us", "zona-2@g.us", "zona-3@g.us")
    assert accounts[1].group_ids == ("zona-4@g.us", "zona-5@g.us", "zona-6@g.us")
    assert wa.find_account_for_chat("zona-5@g.us", accounts).label == "secundaria"


def test_simple_respects_first_account_explicit_groups(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    from app import scheduler

    for key in list(os.environ):
        if key == "GREEN_API_ACCOUNTS" or key.startswith(
            ("GREEN_API_INSTANCE_ID", "GREEN_API_TOKEN", "WHATSAPP_GROUP_IDS", "WA_GROUP_")
        ):
            monkeypatch.delenv(key, raising=False)

    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_INSTANCE_ID", "100")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_TOKEN", "token-principal")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_INSTANCE_ID_2", "200")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_TOKEN_2", "token-secundaria")
    for index, zona in enumerate(scheduler.ZONAS, start=1):
        monkeypatch.setenv(zona["env_key"], f"zona-{index}@g.us")
    # La primera instancia ya estaba en estos 3 grupos, que no son los primeros.
    monkeypatch.setenv("WHATSAPP_GROUP_IDS", "zona-2@g.us,zona-4@g.us,zona-6@g.us")

    accounts = wa.get_accounts()

    assert accounts[0].group_ids == ("zona-2@g.us", "zona-4@g.us", "zona-6@g.us")
    assert accounts[1].group_ids == ("zona-1@g.us", "zona-3@g.us", "zona-5@g.us")


def test_simple_second_account_requires_token(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_INSTANCE_ID", "100")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_TOKEN", "token-principal")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_INSTANCE_ID_2", "200")

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="GREEN_API_TOKEN_2"):
        wa.get_accounts()


def test_get_accounts_json_and_routing(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_ACCOUNTS", _accounts_json())

    accounts = wa.get_accounts()

    assert [account.label for account in accounts] == ["principal", "secundaria"]
    assert wa.find_account_for_chat("g4@g.us", accounts).label == "secundaria"
    assert wa.find_account_for_chat("desconocido@g.us", accounts) is None


def test_duplicate_group_is_assigned_to_first_account(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    raw = json.dumps(
        [
            {"label": "a", "instance_id": "1", "token": "t1", "group_ids": ["g1@g.us"]},
            {
                "label": "b",
                "instance_id": "2",
                "token": "t2",
                "group_ids": ["g1@g.us", "g2@g.us"],
            },
        ]
    )
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_ACCOUNTS", raw)

    accounts = wa.get_accounts()

    assert accounts[0].group_ids == ("g1@g.us",)
    assert accounts[1].group_ids == ("g2@g.us",)


def test_invalid_json_raises(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_ACCOUNTS", "{no-es-json")

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="JSON"):
        wa.get_accounts()


def test_send_red_alert_fans_out_to_all_accounts(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_ACCOUNTS", _accounts_json())
    calls = []

    def fake_send(instance_id, token, chat_id, message, max_retries=3):
        calls.append((instance_id, chat_id))
        return {"idMessage": chat_id}

    monkeypatch.setattr(wa, "_send_message", fake_send)

    results = wa.send_red_alert("grid-1", -7.16, -78.5, 0.9)

    assert calls == [
        ("100", "g1@g.us"),
        ("100", "g2@g.us"),
        ("200", "g3@g.us"),
        ("200", "g4@g.us"),
    ]
    assert all(result["status"] == "ok" for result in results)


def test_send_message_to_chat_routes_and_rejects_unknown(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_ACCOUNTS", _accounts_json())

    def fake_send(instance_id, token, chat_id, message, max_retries=3):
        return {"instance": instance_id, "chat": chat_id}

    monkeypatch.setattr(wa, "_send_message", fake_send)
    accounts = wa.get_accounts()

    result = wa.send_message_to_chat("g4@g.us", "hola", accounts=accounts)
    assert result["account"] == "secundaria"

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="no está asignado"):
        wa.send_message_to_chat("desconocido@g.us", "hola", accounts=accounts)


def test_send_message_error_does_not_leak_token(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    class FakeResponse:
        status_code = 466
        text = '{"error":"group limit","token":"SUPER_SECRETO"}'

        def json(self):
            return {}

    monkeypatch.setattr(wa.requests, "post", lambda *args, **kwargs: FakeResponse())

    with pytest.raises(wa.GreenAPIError) as exc:
        wa._send_message("100", "SUPER_SECRETO", "g1@g.us", "hola", max_retries=1)

    assert "466" in str(exc.value)
    assert "SUPER_SECRETO" not in str(exc.value)


def test_scheduler_routes_each_zone_to_its_account(clean_whatsapp_env, monkeypatch):
    from app import scheduler

    zone_groups = {
        zona["env_key"]: f"zona-{index}@g.us"
        for index, zona in enumerate(scheduler.ZONAS, start=1)
    }
    for key, value in zone_groups.items():
        monkeypatch.setenv(key, value)

    group_ids = list(zone_groups.values())
    raw = json.dumps(
        [
            {
                "label": "principal",
                "instance_id": "100",
                "token": "token-principal",
                "group_ids": group_ids[:3],
            },
            {
                "label": "secundaria",
                "instance_id": "200",
                "token": "token-secundaria",
                "group_ids": group_ids[3:],
            },
        ]
    )
    monkeypatch.setenv("GREEN_API_ACCOUNTS", raw)
    monkeypatch.setattr(scheduler, "_fetch_risk_data", lambda: [{}])
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        scheduler,
        "_classify_zone",
        lambda features, zona: {"nombre": zona["nombre"], "nivel": "Verde"},
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(scheduler, "_build_message", lambda info: "mensaje de prueba")

    calls = []

    def fake_send(chat_id, message, accounts=None):
        account = wa.find_account_for_chat(chat_id, accounts)
        calls.append((chat_id, account.label))
        return {"chat_id": chat_id, "account": account.label, "response": {}}

    monkeypatch.setattr(scheduler, "send_message_to_chat", fake_send)

    asyncio.run(scheduler.run_daily_alerts())

    assert [account for _, account in calls] == [
        "principal",
        "principal",
        "principal",
        "secundaria",
        "secundaria",
        "secundaria",
    ]

"""
audit_quick.py - Resumen ejecutivo de auditoría ISO 25010
"""
import sys, time, warnings
import requests, joblib, numpy as np, pandas as pd
from pathlib import Path

warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
ROOT = Path('.')
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")

BASE_URL = 'http://127.0.0.1:5050'
MODEL_PATH = ROOT / 'models' / 'lightgbm_wildfire.pkl'
WEB_DIR = ROOT / 'web'
APP_DIR = ROOT / 'app'

from app.model import FEATURE_ORDER as FEAT, DEFAULT_VALUES, classify  # 18 features (fuente única en app/model.py)

def g(path, timeout=8, **params):
    try: return requests.get(f'{BASE_URL}{path}', params=params, timeout=timeout)
    except: return None

def p(path, body, timeout=8):
    try: return requests.post(f'{BASE_URL}{path}', json=body, timeout=timeout)
    except: return None

results = {}

print('\n' + '='*72)
print('  AUDITORIA ISO/IEC 25010:2011 — AlertaForestal Peru v2.0')
print('='*72)

# ── 1. ADECUACION FUNCIONAL ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
print('\n[1] ADECUACION FUNCIONAL')
cat = []
for ep,m in [('/health','GET'),('/risk/today','GET'),('/model/importance','GET'),
             ('/social/matrix','GET'),('/alert/status','GET'),('/firms/hotspots','GET')]:
    r = g(ep)
    ok = r is not None and r.status_code < 500
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  {m} {ep}  HTTP {r.status_code if r else "N/A"}')

pl = {'grid_id':'CAJ-001','pendiente_pct':38.0,'orientacion':'NE','ndvi':0.12,
      'dist_carretera_m':120.0,'dist_poblado_m':350.0,'temp_2m':34.5,
      'humedad_relativa':18.0,'viento_velocidad':32.0,'viento_direccion':45.0}
r = p('/predict', pl)
ok = r is not None and r.status_code == 200
cat.append(ok)
if ok:
    d = r.json()
    print(f'  PASA  POST /predict  nivel={d["nivel"]} p={d["probabilidad"]:.4f} percentil={d.get("percentil")}')
    ok2 = d['nivel'] in ('Verde', 'Amarillo', 'Rojo') and d['nivel'] == classify(d['probabilidad'])
    cat.append(ok2)
    print(f'  {"PASA" if ok2 else "FALLA"}  Clasificacion /predict consistente con los umbrales del modelo')

r = g('/risk/today', limit=5)
if r and r.status_code == 200:
    d = r.json()
    f0 = d['features'][0] if d['features'] else {}
    ok = d.get('type') == 'FeatureCollection'
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  GeoJSON type={d.get("type")} count={d.get("count")} geom={f0.get("geometry",{}).get("type")}')

r = p('/propagate?grid_id=-78.5000_-7.1600&viento_direccion=45&viento_velocidad=25', {})
if r and r.status_code == 200:
    d = r.json()
    ok = len(d.get('grillas_afectadas', [])) > 0
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  POST /propagate  afectadas={len(d.get("grillas_afectadas",[]))}')

results['1_Adecuacion_Funcional'] = (sum(cat), len(cat))

# ── 2. EFICIENCIA DE DESEMPENO ────────────────────────────────────────────────
print('\n[2] EFICIENCIA DE DESEMPENO')
cat = []
g('/health')  # warm-up: evita penalizar el primer handshake TCP (keep-alive)
for ep, lim_ms in [('/health', 200), ('/model/importance', 500)]:
    times = []
    for _ in range(5):
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        r = g(ep)
        if r and r.status_code == 200:
            times.append((time.perf_counter()-t0)*1000)
    if times:
        avg = np.mean(times)
        ok = avg < lim_ms
        cat.append(ok)
        print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  {ep}  media={avg:.1f}ms (obj<{lim_ms}ms) p95={np.percentile(times,95):.1f}ms')

model = joblib.load(MODEL_PATH)
np.random.seed(42); N = 1000
RANGOS = {
    'pendiente_pct': (0, 60), 'ndvi': (0.05, 0.9),
    'dist_carretera_m': (50, 10000), 'dist_poblado_m': (100, 15000),
    'temp_2m': (12, 40), 'humedad_relativa': (8, 95),
    'viento_velocidad': (0, 70), 'viento_direccion': (0, 360),
}
df = pd.DataFrame({
    col: (np.random.uniform(*RANGOS[col], N) if col in RANGOS else DEFAULT_VALUES.get(col, 0.0))
    for col in FEAT
})
t0 = time.perf_counter(); _ = model.predict_proba(df[FEAT])[:,1]
ms = (time.perf_counter()-t0)*1000
ms_p = ms/N
ok = ms_p < 2.0
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  ML batch N={N}  total={ms:.2f}ms  por_muestra={ms_p:.4f}ms (obj<2ms)')

# 20 peticiones consecutivas
times_h = []
for _ in range(20):
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    r = g('/health')
    if r and r.status_code == 200:
        times_h.append((time.perf_counter()-t0)*1000)
ok1 = len(times_h)/20 >= 0.95
ok2 = np.percentile(times_h, 95) < 500 if times_h else False
cat.extend([ok1, ok2])
print(f'  {"PASA" if ok1 else "FALLA"}  20 peticiones /health: {len(times_h)}/20 exitosas')
print(f'  {"PASA" if ok2 else "FALLA"}  P95 latencia /health: {np.percentile(times_h,95):.1f}ms (obj<500ms)')

results['2_Eficiencia_Desempeno'] = (sum(cat), len(cat))

# ── 3. COMPATIBILIDAD ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
print('\n[3] COMPATIBILIDAD')
cat = []
# CORS restringido: se refleja un origen permitido y se bloquea uno ajeno.
ALLOWED_ORIGIN = 'http://127.0.0.1:5050'
FOREIGN_ORIGIN = 'http://evil.example'
try:
    r_allowed = requests.get(f'{BASE_URL}/health', headers={'Origin': ALLOWED_ORIGIN}, timeout=8)
    cors = r_allowed.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin', '')
    r_foreign = requests.get(f'{BASE_URL}/health', headers={'Origin': FOREIGN_ORIGIN}, timeout=8)
    foreign_cors = r_foreign.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin', '')
    ok = (cors == ALLOWED_ORIGIN) and (foreign_cors == '')
except Exception:
    ok, cors = False, ''
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  CORS restringido: permitido="{cors}" | ajeno bloqueado')

for ep in ['/health', '/risk/today?limit=2', '/model/importance']:
    r = g(ep)
    ct = r.headers.get('content-type','') if r and r.status_code==200 else ''
    ok = 'application/json' in ct
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  Content-Type JSON en {ep}: "{ct}"')

r = g('/docs')
ok = r is not None and r.status_code == 200
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  Swagger /docs: HTTP {r.status_code if r else "N/A"}')

r = g('/openapi.json')
ok = r is not None and r.status_code == 200
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  OpenAPI JSON: HTTP {r.status_code if r else "N/A"}')

r2 = g('/risk/today', limit=3)
if r2 and r2.status_code == 200:
    d = r2.json()
    ok = d.get('type') == 'FeatureCollection' and bool(d.get('features'))
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  GeoJSON RFC 7946 FeatureCollection valido')

results['3_Compatibilidad'] = (sum(cat), len(cat))

    cat.append(ok)

results['6_Seguridad'] = (sum(cat), len(cat))

# ── 7. MANTENIBILIDAD ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
print('\n[7] MANTENIBILIDAD')
cat = []
for d in ['app','web','data_pipeline','airflow','dashboard','data','models','tests']:
    ok = (ROOT/d).exists()

    print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  Directorio /{d}')
for f in ['main.py','model.py','propagation.py','scheduler.py','pdf_generator.py','whatsapp_alerts.py']:
    ok = (APP_DIR/f).exists()
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  app/{f}')
mc = (APP_DIR/'main.py').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
ok = 'os.getenv' in mc
cat.append(ok); print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  main.py usa os.getenv()')
req = ROOT / 'requirements.txt'
if req.exists():
    lines = [l for l in req.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines() if l.strip() and not l.startswith('#')]
    ok = len(lines) >= 10
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  requirements.txt: {len(lines)} dependencias')

df_path = ROOT / 'Dockerfile'

    for instr in ['FROM','WORKDIR','COPY','CMD']:
        ok = instr in dc
        cat.append(ok)
        print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  Dockerfile: {instr}')
parquet = ROOT/'data'/'dataset.parquet'
csv_f = ROOT/'data'/'dataset.csv'

print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  dataset.parquet ({parquet.stat().st_size/1024/1024:.1f}MB)' if ok else '  FALLA  dataset.parquet')
ok2 = csv_f.exists()
cat.append(ok2)
print(f'  {"PASA" if ok2 else "FALLA"}  dataset.csv ({csv_f.stat().st_size/1024/1024:.1f}MB)' if ok2 else '  FALLA  dataset.csv')
r = g('/')
ok = r is not None and r.status_code == 200 and 'text/html' in r.headers.get('content-type','')
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  FastAPI sirve portal web en /: text/html={ok}')
req_c = req.read_text(encoding='utf-8').lower() if req.exists() else ''
for pkg,desc in [('fastapi','API'),('lightgbm','ML'),('geopandas','geo'),('locust','carga'),('apscheduler','scheduler')]:
    ok = pkg in req_c
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  requirements incluye {pkg} ({desc})')
for f in ['main.py','model.py']:
    c = (APP_DIR/f).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    ok = 'from pathlib' in c or 'Path(' in c
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"PASA" if ok else "FALLA"}  app/{f} usa pathlib (cross-platform)')

results['8_Portabilidad'] = (sum(cat), len(cat))

print('\n' + '='*72)
print('  RESUMEN EJECUTIVO — ISO/IEC 25010:2011')
print('='*72)
total_p = 0; total_t = 0
names = {
    '1_Adecuacion_Funcional': '1. Adecuacion Funcional',
    '2_Eficiencia_Desempeno': '2. Eficiencia de Desempeno',
    '3_Compatibilidad':       '3. Compatibilidad',
    '4_Usabilidad':           '4. Usabilidad',
    '5_Fiabilidad':           '5. Fiabilidad',
    '6_Seguridad':            '6. Seguridad',
    '7_Mantenibilidad':       '7. Mantenibilidad',
    '8_Portabilidad':         '8. Portabilidad',
}
print(f'\n  {"Caracteristica":<32} {"Pasa":>5} {"Total":>6} {"Pct":>7}  Calificacion')
print('  ' + '-'*65)
for key, name in names.items():
    ps, tt = results.get(key, (0,0))
    pct = ps/tt*100 if tt else 0
    total_p += ps; total_t += tt
    grade = ('EXCELENTE' if pct>=90 else 'BUENO' if pct>=75 else 'ACEPTABLE' if pct>=60 else 'CRITICO')
    print(f'  {name:<32} {ps:>5}/{tt:<5} {pct:>6.1f}%  {grade}')
print('  ' + '-'*65)
gpct = total_p/total_t*100 if total_t else 0
print(f'  {"TOTAL GLOBAL":<32} {total_p:>5}/{total_t:<5} {gpct:>6.1f}%  {"APROBADO" if gpct>=80 else "CONDICIONAL" if gpct>=60 else "REPROBADO"}')
print('='*72)
print(f'  VEREDICTO: {"SISTEMA APROBADO ISO/IEC 25010" if gpct>=80 else "SISTEMA REQUIERE MEJORAS"}')
print('='*72 + '\n')

"""
audit_final.py — Auditoría ISO 25010 sin llamadas a APIs externas lentas
"""
import sys, time, warnings, re
from pathlib import Path
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
ROOT = Path('.')
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")

import requests, joblib, numpy as np, pandas as pd

BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:5050'
MODEL_PATH = ROOT / 'models' / 'lightgbm_wildfire.pkl'
WEB = ROOT / 'web'
APP = ROOT / 'app'
from app.model import FEATURE_ORDER as FEAT, DEFAULT_VALUES, THRESHOLDS  # 18 features (fuente única en app/model.py)
T = 5

def g(path, **params):
    try: return requests.get(f'{BASE}{path}', params=params, timeout=T)
    except: return None

def po(path, body):
    try: return requests.post(f'{BASE}{path}', json=body, timeout=T)
    except: return None

results = {}

print('\n' + '='*72)
print('  AUDITORIA ISO/IEC 25010:2011 — AlertaForestal Peru v2.0')
print('='*72)

# ─── 1. ADECUACION FUNCIONAL ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
print('\n[1] ADECUACION FUNCIONAL')
cat = []
# Endpoints que NO llaman a NASA en vivo
for ep in ['/health', '/risk/today', '/model/importance', '/social/matrix', '/alert/status']:
    x = g(ep)
    ok = x is not None and x.status_code < 500
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  GET {ep}  HTTP {x.status_code if x else "N/A"}')

from app.model import classify
# /predict: la clasificación debe ser consistente con los umbrales operativos del modelo
pl = {'grid_id': 'CAJ-001', 'pendiente_pct': 38.0, 'orientacion': 'NE', 'ndvi': 0.12,
      'dist_carretera_m': 120.0, 'dist_poblado_m': 350.0, 'temp_2m': 34.5,
      'humedad_relativa': 18.0, 'viento_velocidad': 32.0, 'viento_direccion': 45.0}
x = po('/predict', pl)
ok_predict = x is not None and x.status_code == 200
cat.append(ok_predict)
if ok_predict:
    d = x.json()
    ok_prob = 0.0 <= d['probabilidad'] <= 1.0
    ok_clas = d['nivel'] in ('Verde', 'Amarillo', 'Rojo') and d['nivel'] == classify(d['probabilidad'])
    cat.extend([ok_prob, ok_clas])
    print(f'  OK    POST /predict  nivel={d["nivel"]} p={d["probabilidad"]:.4f} percentil={d.get("percentil")}')
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok_prob else "FAIL"}  probabilidad en [0,1]')
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok_clas else "FAIL"}  Clasificación consistente con umbrales del modelo')
else:
    cat.extend([False, False])
    print(f'  FAIL  POST /predict')

# La lógica de umbrales (fuente única en app.model) debe particionar correctamente
ok_u1 = classify(THRESHOLDS['rojo']) == 'Rojo'
ok_u2 = classify(THRESHOLDS['amarillo']) == 'Amarillo'
ok_u3 = classify(0.0) == 'Verde'
cat.extend([ok_u1, ok_u2, ok_u3])
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok_u1 else "FAIL"}  classify(p99) == Rojo')
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok_u2 else "FAIL"}  classify(p95) == Amarillo')
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok_u3 else "FAIL"}  classify(0.0) == Verde')

# GeoJSON
x = g('/risk/today', limit=5)
if x and x.status_code == 200:
    d = x.json()
    ok = d.get('type') == 'FeatureCollection' and len(d.get('features', [])) > 0
    cat.append(ok)
    f0 = d['features'][0] if d['features'] else {}
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  GeoJSON type={d.get("type")} count={d.get("count")} geom={f0.get("geometry",{}).get("type")}')
    props = f0.get('properties', {})
    ok2 = 'nivel' in props and 'probabilidad' in props
    cat.append(ok2)
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok2 else "FAIL"}  Propiedades nivel+probabilidad presentes')
else:
    cat.extend([False, False])

# Motor de propagacion
x = po('/propagate?grid_id=-78.5000_-7.1600&viento_direccion=45&viento_velocidad=25', {})
if x and x.status_code == 200:
    d = x.json()
    ok = len(d.get('grillas_afectadas', [])) > 0
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  POST /propagate  afectadas={len(d.get("grillas_afectadas",[]))}')
else:
    cat.append(False)

results['1_Adecuacion_Funcional'] = (sum(cat), len(cat))
print(f'  >>> {sum(cat)}/{len(cat)} ({sum(cat)/len(cat)*100:.1f}%)')

# ─── 2. EFICIENCIA DE DESEMPENO ───────────────────────────────────────────────
print('\n[2] EFICIENCIA DE DESEMPENO')
cat = []

for ep, lim_ms in [('/health', 200), ('/model/importance', 800), ('/alert/status', 300)]:
    times = []
    for _ in range(5):
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        x = g(ep)
        if x and x.status_code == 200:
            times.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
    if times:
        avg = np.mean(times)
        ok = avg < lim_ms
        cat.append(ok)
        print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  {ep}  media={avg:.1f}ms  p95={np.percentile(times,95):.1f}ms  (obj<{lim_ms}ms)')

# ML batch 1000
model = joblib.load(MODEL_PATH)
np.random.seed(42)
N = 1000
RANGOS = {
    'pendiente_pct': (0, 60), 'ndvi': (0.05, 0.9),
    'dist_carretera_m': (50, 10000), 'dist_poblado_m': (100, 15000),
    'temp_2m': (12, 40), 'humedad_relativa': (8, 95),
    'viento_velocidad': (0, 70), 'viento_direccion': (0, 360),
}
df_batch = pd.DataFrame({
    col: (np.random.uniform(*RANGOS[col], N) if col in RANGOS else DEFAULT_VALUES.get(col, 0.0))
    for col in FEAT
})
t0 = time.perf_counter()
probas_batch = model.predict_proba(df_batch[FEAT])[:, 1]
ms_total = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
ms_per = ms_total / N
ok = ms_per < 2.0
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  ML batch N={N}  total={ms_total:.2f}ms  por_muestra={ms_per:.4f}ms  (obj<2ms)')

# Distribucion de niveles (umbrales data-driven de app/model.py)
rojas    = int((probas_batch >= THRESHOLDS["rojo"]).sum())
amarillas = int(((probas_batch >= THRESHOLDS["amarillo"]) & (probas_batch < THRESHOLDS["rojo"])).sum())
verdes   = int((probas_batch < THRESHOLDS["amarillo"]).sum())
print(f'  INFO  Distribucion: Rojo={rojas}({rojas/N:.1%}) Amarillo={amarillas}({amarillas/N:.1%}) Verde={verdes}({verdes/N:.1%})')
print(f'  INFO  Stats p: media={probas_batch.mean():.4f} mediana={np.median(probas_batch):.4f} std={probas_batch.std():.4f}')

# 20 peticiones consecutivas
times_20 = []
for _ in range(20):
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    x = g('/health')
    if x and x.status_code == 200:
        times_20.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
ok_rate = len(times_20) / 20 >= 0.95
ok_p95  = np.percentile(times_20, 95) < 500 if times_20 else False
cat.extend([ok_rate, ok_p95])
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok_rate else "FAIL"}  Tasa exito 20x /health: {len(times_20)}/20')
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok_p95 else "FAIL"}  P95 latencia: {np.percentile(times_20,95):.1f}ms (obj<500ms)')

results['2_Eficiencia_Desempeno'] = (sum(cat), len(cat))
print(f'  >>> {sum(cat)}/{len(cat)} ({sum(cat)/len(cat)*100:.1f}%)')

# ─── 3. COMPATIBILIDAD ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
print('\n[3] COMPATIBILIDAD')
cat = []

# CORS restringido: se refleja un origen permitido y se bloquea uno ajeno.
ALLOWED_ORIGIN = 'http://127.0.0.1:5050'
FOREIGN_ORIGIN = 'http://evil.example'
try:
    x_allowed = requests.get(f'{BASE}/health', headers={'Origin': ALLOWED_ORIGIN}, timeout=8)
    cors = x_allowed.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin', '')
    x_foreign = requests.get(f'{BASE}/health', headers={'Origin': FOREIGN_ORIGIN}, timeout=8)
    foreign_cors = x_foreign.headers.get('access-control-allow-origin', '')
    ok = (cors == ALLOWED_ORIGIN) and (foreign_cors == '')
except Exception:
    ok, cors = False, ''
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  CORS restringido: permitido="{cors}" | ajeno bloqueado')

for ep in ['/health', '/model/importance', '/social/matrix']:
    x = g(ep)
    ct = x.headers.get('content-type', '') if x and x.status_code == 200 else ''
    ok = 'application/json' in ct
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  Content-Type JSON en {ep}')

x = g('/docs')
ok = x is not None and x.status_code == 200
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  Swagger /docs HTTP {x.status_code if x else "N/A"}')

x = g('/openapi.json')
ok = x is not None and x.status_code == 200
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  OpenAPI JSON HTTP {x.status_code if x else "N/A"}')

if x and x.status_code == 200:
    schema = x.json()

    ok = 'alerta' in title.lower() or 'wildfire' in title.lower()
    cat.append(ok)

    endpoints_count = len(schema.get('paths', {}))
    ok2 = endpoints_count >= 8
    cat.append(ok2)
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok2 else "FAIL"}  OpenAPI documenta >={8} endpoints (tiene {endpoints_count})')

# GeoJSON RFC 7946
x = g('/risk/today', limit=3)
if x and x.status_code == 200:

    geom_type = f0.get('geometry', {}).get('type', '')

    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  Geometria GeoJSON valida: "{geom_type}"')

results['3_Compatibilidad'] = (sum(cat), len(cat))

pl_rel = {'grid_id': 'R', 'pendiente_pct': 20.0, 'orientacion': 'NE', 'ndvi': 0.30,

for label, body in [
    ('Temp extrema 60C', {'grid_id': 'E1', 'pendiente_pct': 20, 'orientacion': 'N', 'ndvi': 0.3,

]:

    if x and x.status_code == 200:
        pv = x.json().get('probabilidad', -1)
        ok = 0.0 <= pv <= 1.0

        print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  Tolerancia {label}: p={pv:.4f} HTTP {x.status_code}')
    else:
        cat.append(False)
        print(f'  FAIL  Tolerancia {label}: HTTP {x.status_code if x else "N/A"}')

try:

    no_ver = not re.search(r'\d+\.\d+', srv)
    cat.append(no_ver)

for path in ['/.env', '/.env.example', '/.git/config']:
    x = g(path)

    cat.append(ok)

                     'orientacion': 'N', 'ndvi': 0.3, 'dist_carretera_m': 400,
                     'dist_poblado_m': 800, 'temp_2m': 28, 'humedad_relativa': 32,
                     'viento_velocidad': 20, 'viento_direccion': 45})
ok = x is not None and x.status_code in [200, 422]
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  SQL injection en grid_id: HTTP {x.status_code if x else "N/A"}')

x = po('/predict', {})
ok = x is not None and x.status_code == 422
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  Payload vacío → 422: HTTP {x.status_code if x else "N/A"}')

env_ex = ROOT / '.env.example'
if env_ex.exists():
    c = env_ex.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    real = any(k in c for k in ['a86632ae', 'bd46e923', 'minioadmin'])
    ok = not real
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  .env.example sin credenciales reales: {"limpio" if ok else "CREDENCIALES EXPUESTAS"}')

main_c = (APP / 'main.py').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
real_in_code = 'a86632ae' in main_c
ok = not real_in_code
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  main.py sin API keys hardcodeadas: {"limpio" if ok else "KEY EXPUESTA EN CODIGO"}')

results['6_Seguridad'] = (sum(cat), len(cat))
print(f'  >>> {sum(cat)}/{len(cat)} ({sum(cat)/len(cat)*100:.1f}%)')

# ─── 7. MANTENIBILIDAD ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
print('\n[7] MANTENIBILIDAD')
cat = []

dirs_ok = 0
for d in ['app', 'web', 'data_pipeline', 'airflow', 'dashboard', 'data', 'models', 'tests']:
    ok = (ROOT / d).exists()
    dirs_ok += ok
    cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if dirs_ok==8 else "PARCIAL"}  Directorios de proyecto: {dirs_ok}/8')

app_files_ok = 0
for f in ['main.py', 'model.py', 'propagation.py', 'scheduler.py', 'pdf_generator.py', 'whatsapp_alerts.py']:
    ok = (APP / f).exists()
    app_files_ok += ok
    cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if app_files_ok==6 else "PARCIAL"}  Módulos app/: {app_files_ok}/6 presentes')

main_c = (APP / 'main.py').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
ok = 'os.getenv' in main_c
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  main.py usa os.getenv() para configuracion')

for fname in ['main.py', 'model.py', 'propagation.py']:
    c = (APP / fname).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    ok = '"""' in c or "'''" in c
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  app/{fname} tiene docstrings')

req = ROOT / 'requirements.txt'
lines = [l for l in req.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines() if l.strip() and not l.startswith('#')] if req.exists() else []
ok = len(lines) >= 10
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  requirements.txt: {len(lines)} dependencias')

has_ver = any('>=' in l or '==' in l for l in lines)
cat.append(has_ver)
print(f'  {"OK  " if has_ver else "FAIL"}  Dependencias con versiones mínimas (>=)')

ok = (ROOT / '.env.example').exists()
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  .env.example para onboarding')

ok = (ROOT / 'docker-compose.yml').exists()
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  docker-compose.yml presente (IaC)')

ok = (ROOT / 'Dockerfile').exists()
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  Dockerfile presente')

tests_found = list((ROOT / 'tests').glob('*.py'))
ok = len(tests_found) >= 3
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  Suite de tests: {len(tests_found)} archivos (.py)')

results['7_Mantenibilidad'] = (sum(cat), len(cat))
print(f'  >>> {sum(cat)}/{len(cat)} ({sum(cat)/len(cat)*100:.1f}%)')

# ─── 8. PORTABILIDAD ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
print('\n[8] PORTABILIDAD')
cat = []

df_path = ROOT / 'Dockerfile'
if df_path.exists():
    dc = df_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    for instr in ['FROM', 'WORKDIR', 'COPY', 'CMD']:
        ok = instr in dc
        cat.append(ok)
        print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  Dockerfile: instruccion {instr}')

pq = ROOT / 'data' / 'dataset.parquet'
csv_f = ROOT / 'data' / 'dataset.csv'
ok = pq.exists()
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  dataset.parquet ({pq.stat().st_size/1024/1024:.1f} MB)' if ok else '  FAIL  dataset.parquet no existe')
ok2 = csv_f.exists()
cat.append(ok2)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok2 else "FAIL"}  dataset.csv ({csv_f.stat().st_size/1024/1024:.1f} MB)' if ok2 else '  FAIL  dataset.csv no existe')

x = g('/')
ok = x is not None and x.status_code == 200 and 'text/html' in x.headers.get('content-type', '')
cat.append(ok)
print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  FastAPI sirve HTML en / (sin servidor externo)')

req_c = req.read_text(encoding='utf-8').lower() if req.exists() else ''
for pkg, desc in [('fastapi','API REST'), ('lightgbm','ML'), ('geopandas','Geoespacial'),
                   ('locust','Carga'), ('apscheduler','Alertas automáticas'), ('streamlit','Dashboard')]:
    ok = pkg in req_c
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  requirements.txt incluye {pkg} ({desc})')

for f in ['main.py', 'model.py', 'propagation.py']:
    c = (APP / f).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    usa_pathlib = 'from pathlib' in c or 'Path(' in c
    tiene_acceso_archivos = any(t in c for t in ('open(', 'read_text', 'read_csv', 'joblib.load', 'pickle.load'))
    sin_rutas_os = ('C:\\' not in c) and ('/home/' not in c) and ('/Users/' not in c)
    # Portabilidad: sin rutas absolutas OS-especificas; usa pathlib cuando accede a archivos
    ok = sin_rutas_os and (usa_pathlib or not tiene_acceso_archivos)
    cat.append(ok)
    print(f'  {"OK  " if ok else "FAIL"}  app/{f} portabilidad de rutas (pathlib / sin rutas OS)')

results['8_Portabilidad'] = (sum(cat), len(cat))
print(f'  >>> {sum(cat)}/{len(cat)} ({sum(cat)/len(cat)*100:.1f}%)')

# ─── RESUMEN FINAL ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
print('\n' + '='*72)
print('  RESUMEN EJECUTIVO — ISO/IEC 25010:2011')
print('='*72)
NAMES = {
    '1_Adecuacion_Funcional': '1. Adecuacion Funcional',
    '2_Eficiencia_Desempeno': '2. Eficiencia de Desempeno',
    '3_Compatibilidad':       '3. Compatibilidad',
    '4_Usabilidad':           '4. Usabilidad',
    '5_Fiabilidad':           '5. Fiabilidad',
    '6_Seguridad':            '6. Seguridad',
    '7_Mantenibilidad':       '7. Mantenibilidad',
    '8_Portabilidad':         '8. Portabilidad',
}
total_p = 0
total_t = 0
print(f'\n  {"Caracteristica":<30} {"OK":>4}/{"Tot":<4}  {"Pct":>6}  Nivel')
print('  ' + '-'*62)
for key, name in NAMES.items():
    ps, tot = results.get(key, (0, 0))
    pct = ps / tot * 100 if tot else 0
    total_p += ps; total_t += tot
    if pct >= 90:   grade = 'EXCELENTE  [*****]'
    elif pct >= 75: grade = 'BUENO      [****]'
    elif pct >= 60: grade = 'ACEPTABLE  [***]'
    else:           grade = 'MEJORABLE  [**]'
    print(f'  {name:<30} {ps:>4}/{tot:<4}  {pct:>6.1f}%  {grade}')
print('  ' + '-'*62)
gpct = total_p / total_t * 100 if total_t else 0
verdict = 'SISTEMA APROBADO' if gpct >= 80 else 'CONDICIONAL (>60%)' if gpct >= 60 else 'REPROBADO'
print(f'  {"TOTAL GLOBAL":<30} {total_p:>4}/{total_t:<4}  {gpct:>6.1f}%  {verdict}')
print('='*72)
print(f'\n  Fecha: {__import__("datetime").datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")}')
print(f'  Sistema: AlertaForestal Peru v2.0  |  URL: {BASE}')
print(f'  Norma: ISO/IEC 25010:2011\n')
